public class Solution {
    public Dictionary<int,int>OddDict= new Dictionary<int,int>();
    public Dictionary<int,int>EvenDict= new Dictionary<int,int>();
    public int[]Nums = [];
    public int LongestBalanced(int[] nums) {

        Nums = nums;
        for(int i = 0; i<nums.Length;i++){
            var num = nums[i];
            if(num%2==0){
                if(EvenDict.TryGetValue(num,out var count)){
                    EvenDict[num]=count+1;
                }else{
                    EvenDict.Add(num,1);
                }
            }else{
                if(OddDict.TryGetValue(num,out var count)){
                    OddDict[num]=count+1;
                }else{
                    OddDict.Add(num,1);
                }
            }
        }
        if(OddDict.Count()==EvenDict.Count()){
            return nums.Length;
        }
        if(OddDict.Count()==0 || EvenDict.Count()==0){
            return 0;
        }
        var maxFromLeft = GetLongest(1,nums.Length-1,nums[0]);

        var maxFromRigth = GetLongest(0,nums.Length-2,nums[^1]);
        return Math.Max(maxFromRigth,maxFromLeft);



    }
    public int GetLongest(int start,int end,int num){
    //removing and counting
        (int key,int value)? removedEven = null;
        (int key,int value)? removedOdd = null;
        var maxLength = -1;
        if(num%2==0){
            var found =EvenDict.TryGetValue(num,out var count);
            removedEven = (num,count);
            if(found && count>1){
                EvenDict[num]= count-1;
            }else if(found && count == 1){
                EvenDict.Remove(num);
            }else if(!found){
                throw new Exception("dict can not have num");
            }
            
        }else if (num%2!=0){
            var found =OddDict.TryGetValue(num,out var count);
            removedOdd = (num,count);
            if(found && count>1){
                OddDict[num]= count-1;
            }else if(found && count == 1){
                OddDict.Remove(num);
            }else if(!found){
                throw new Exception("dict can not have num");
            }
        }

        if(OddDict.Count() == EvenDict.Count()){
            maxLength = end - start+1;
        }

        if(removedEven != null){
            EvenDict[removedEven.Value.key]= removedEven.Value.value;
        }else if (removedOdd != null){
            OddDict[removedOdd.Value.key]= removedOdd.Value.value;
        }
        if(maxLength== -1){
            if(end - (start+1)!=0){
                var maxFromLeft = GetLongest(start+1,end,Nums[start]);
                var maxFromRigth = GetLongest(start,end-1,Nums[end]);
                maxLength = Math.Max(maxFromLeft,maxFromRigth);
            }
           
        }

        return maxLength;
    }
}
